===== MAKE IOS =====
[make exit=0]
--- last 25 lines of make-ios.out ---
## left empty -- grammars cannot be installed at runtime on iOS
## (see ios/README).
mkdir -p /Users/runner/work/emacs/emacs/build-ios-sim/ios/Emacs.app/tree-sitter
if [ -d "../../ios/Emacs.base/tree-sitter" ]; then \
	  cp -f ../../ios/Emacs.base/tree-sitter/*.dylib \
	    /Users/runner/work/emacs/emacs/build-ios-sim/ios/Emacs.app/tree-sitter/ 2>/dev/null || true; \
	fi
## DOC (built-in docstrings) is generated into cross/etc by
## the top-level ios target; without it C-h on any C-level
## function or variable fails.  The Info manuals land in
## <srcdir>/info via make info.  ios.m points EMACSDOC and
## INFOPATH at these bundle locations.
touch /Users/runner/work/emacs/emacs/build-ios-sim/ios/Emacs.app/etc/charsets || true
cp -f /Users/runner/work/emacs/emacs/build-ios-sim/cross/src/emacs /Users/runner/work/emacs/emacs/build-ios-sim/ios/Emacs.app/emacs
## actool reports errors on stdout and can exit 0 while doing
## so; the icon step below therefore logs actool's output and
## treats the run as failed unless the compiled Assets.car
## landed in the bundle and the partial plist parses, printing
## the log in that case.  (The icon keys sit nested under
## CFBundleIcons/CFBundlePrimaryIcon in the partial plist, so
## probing a top-level key is not a valid success check.)
ios/Makefile: no AppIcon.png; skipping asset catalog
  (App Store submissions require one; see ios/README)
ios/Makefile: CODESIGN_IDENTITY unset; producing
  unsigned bundle suitable for the Simulator only.
--- make errors (if any) ---

--- ios-test-results.txt ---
PASS sandbox-paths-callable -- VFS primitives return non-nil paths
PASS home-is-documents -- HOME equals ios-documents-directory
PASS cwd-is-documents -- Current working directory is the documents tree
PASS file-roundtrip -- find-file / write / re-read round-trips through Documents
PASS clipboard-roundtrip -- Lisp -> UIPasteboard -> Lisp preserves text
PASS display-geometry -- x-display-pixel-{width,height} report a real display
PASS appearance-callable -- ios-system-appearance returns dark or light
PASS image-load-png -- create-image on a 1x1 PNG returns a usable image object
PASS face-color-roundtrip -- set-face-attribute followed by face-attribute returns input
PASS frame-bg-mode-set -- frame-background-mode is one of light / dark
PASS color-names-resolve -- named X11 colors resolve through the bridged color table
PASS theme-face-specs-match -- color face-spec display predicates match on the iOS frame
PASS frame-bar-parameters-numeric -- menu/tab/tool-bar-lines frame parameters are numbers
PASS libxml-available -- libxml2 HTML/XML parsing primitives are present
PASS treesit-runtime-available -- the tree-sitter parsing runtime is linked in
PASS sqlite-available -- sqlite3 is linked and an in-memory round-trip works
PASS font-parameter-is-string -- the `font' frame parameter is a name string, not a font object
PASS font-families-multiple -- the Core Text driver lists more than one font family
PASS font-bold-italic-real -- bold and italic resolve to actual fonts, not synthesised flags
PASS char-coverage-cjk -- a CJK ideograph is displayable through the script fontset fallback
PASS char-coverage-emoji -- an emoji is displayable through the Apple Color Emoji fallback
PASS char-coverage-bengali -- Bengali resolves through the script fontset fallback
PASS char-coverage-telugu -- Telugu resolves through the script fontset fallback
PASS font-named-family-opens -- a named family (Courier) resolves to a real font
PASS font-default-is-fixed-pitch -- the default face renders fixed-pitch: i and W measure the same
PASS font-variable-pitch-is-proportional -- variable-pitch renders proportional: W is wider than i
PASS drag-n-drop-handler-bound -- [drag-n-drop] is bound globally to a callable
PASS pinch-event-handler-bound -- [pinch] is bound globally so two-finger pinches do something
PASS wheel-event-handler-bound -- [wheel-up] / [wheel-down] are bound globally
PASS tls-available -- gnutls-available-p reports the linked GnuTLS stack
PASS tls-trust-anchors -- bundled ca-bundle.pem exists and gnutls-trustfiles points at it
PASS doc-strings-available -- built-in docstrings resolve through the bundled DOC file
PASS info-manuals-present -- bundled Info directory exists and INFOPATH points at it
